# Bug register

The single list of every AmiWind bug and its current status. How tracking
works, the report template and the rules: [bug tracking](bugs/README.md).
Dated events are in the [journal](BUG_JOURNAL.md); full reports are in
[docs/bugs](bugs/README.md). CHIM engine bugs by part, with the build each was found
in: [CHIM Engine tracker](bugs/CHIM_TRACKER.md).

## Fix index

Generated from [`docs/bugs/bugs.json`](bugs/bugs.json): do not edit this table
by hand. Change the JSON (or use `tools/bug_register.py add` / `set`), then run
`tools/bug_register.py render`. A test fails if the two disagree.

`fixed` means a correction shipped in the named version with supporting
verification; candidates and mitigations stay `open`. `closed` means not a bug,
a duplicate or superseded (the status says which).

<!-- BEGIN GENERATED BUG REGISTER: edit docs/bugs/bugs.json, then run tools/bug_register.py render -->

839 bugs: 735 open, 95 fixed, 9 closed.

### Persisting, not resolved

Bugs reported two or more times. Most reports first. Each has its report lines on its page.

- [ENGINE-CRASH-EXIT-KEYBOARD-36](bugs/ENGINE-CRASH-EXIT-KEYBOARD-36.md): After AmiWind crashes, the AmigaDOS shell mangles typing: shift/caps latched, M and N never appear (reported 6 times, open)
- [WATER-GAMEPLAY-PARITY-38](bugs/WATER-GAMEPLAY-PARITY-38.md): Water gameplay lacks complete original behavior verification (reported 6 times, open)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-SERIAL-36.md): The image step's stair walk runs one map per worker: a single heavy room keeps one core busy for 13-19 minutes while the others sit idle (reported 5 times, fixed)
- [BUILD-PIPELINE-UNDERUTILIZATION-36](bugs/BUILD-PIPELINE-UNDERUTILIZATION-36.md): Umbrella: several builder phases run on one core while the rest of the machine idles, and some do work the build no longer needs (reported 5 times, open)
- [PLAYER-TORCH-THIRDPERSON-38](bugs/PLAYER-TORCH-THIRDPERSON-38.md): Player torch and holding pose disappear in third person while light remains (reported 5 times, open)
- [ANIMKIT-COVERAGE-35](bugs/ANIMKIT-COVERAGE-35.md): NPCs that move or fight have no walk, run or attack animation: Fargoth and other Seyda Neen villagers glitch toward the player (reported 4 times, open)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-WALK-UNCACHED-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-WALK-UNCACHED-36.md): CHIM frames are stair-walked again on every build: about 69 minutes for 270 frames (reported 4 times, fixed)
- [BUILD-OPTIMIZER-CACHE-KEY-BROAD-36](bugs/BUILD-OPTIMIZER-CACHE-KEY-BROAD-36.md): Regression: the image step's BSP optimizer pass cache reused 13 of 2,658 maps; its key covers the whole image tool, so any image-code change redoes every map (reported 4 times, open)

Retained times show host submission/end-marker polling, not exact guest execution. This is not evidence of missing punch or Preview-specific suppression. Prior scoped evidence remains; queue-drained physical-input observation and full presentation/damage acceptance remain pending. No gameplay repair or new defect inferred. 14 October scoped observation on unchanged delivered dev1: male Argonian physical LMB in ordinary third person and separate side Preview reaches attack state3/body102-103 with visible arm motion and warp off. Physical LMB produces stationary attack state3/body86-87 of106; physical D plus LMB produces state3/body97 with nonzero normal WALK velocity. Original captured frames show arm changes. - [BUILD-PASS-KEY-RULE-NO-CARRYOVER-36](bugs/BUILD-PASS-KEY-RULE-NO-CARRYOVER-36.md): A pass key rule change carried no entries written by older trees: dev4 recomputed 2,372 optimizer maps and the exterior sky maps with code and outputs identical to dev3 (open)

- [BUILD-REUSE-CUTOFF-MODE-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-CUTOFF-MODE-36.md): Early cutoff compared file modes with their write bits: a stage reused by hard link (read-only) never matched the same bytes written fresh, so 10 stages after dev3's bsp rerun were refused (open)

- [ENGINE-UPSTREAM-ASM-UNUSED-36](bugs/ENGINE-UPSTREAM-ASM-UNUSED-36.md): The engine is built without AmiQuake's 68k assembly: spans, fixed point and maths run as C at -O1 (open)
- [FOG-TOWN-HEAVY-32](bugs/FOG-TOWN-HEAVY-32.md): Heavy fog at the Vivec Arena and in Balmora in v0.0.32-dev1 (open)
- [IMAGE-ONE-HDF-TARGET-36](bugs/IMAGE-ONE-HDF-TARGET-36.md): The game ships on two drive images and three world partitions; the project target is the whole game on one HDF (open)
- [INTERIOR-HULL-CHAIN-33](bugs/INTERIOR-HULL-CHAIN-33.md): The Arena Pit's main structure collides through one chain of 36,545 clipnodes; every trace in the room walks it (open)
- [MINIWIND-NOT-MINUTES-33](bugs/MINIWIND-NOT-MINUTES-33.md): A reused MiniWind build took about two hours instead of minutes (open)
- [NPC-BAKED-WHOLE-DUPLICATION-33](bugs/NPC-BAKED-WHOLE-DUPLICATION-33.md): Every NPC appearance is baked whole: shared body parts are converted again for each actor, and equipment cannot change (open)
- PERF-READAHEAD-29: Outdoor cell/sub-cell crossing pauses and lost read-ahead (open)
- [REMOTE-CONSOLE-LOG-COST-32](bugs/REMOTE-CONSOLE-LOG-COST-32.md): With the remote console on, every console line costs about 7-18 ms in FS-UAE (open)
- [TOWN-VIS-OCCLUSION-31](bugs/TOWN-VIS-OCCLUSION-31.md): Converted buildings, rooms and rocks do not block Quake visibility (func_wall) (open)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-WALK-UNCACHED-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-WALK-UNCACHED-36.md): CHIM frames are stair-walked again on every build: about 69 minutes for 270 frames (fixed)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-HASH-UNDERUSED-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-HASH-UNDERUSED-36.md): The image step hashes 34,592 payload files on 3 of 24 cores (one file per pool task) (fixed)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-HEAP-AUDIT-UNDERUSED-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-HEAP-AUDIT-UNDERUSED-36.md): The image step's world map heap audit estimates 170 maps on 5.3 of 24 cores (fixed)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-SERIAL-36.md): The image step's stair walk runs one map per worker: a single heavy room keeps one core busy for 13-19 minutes while the others sit idle (fixed)
- [BUILD-PROFILER-NO-WASTE-ALARM-36](bugs/BUILD-PROFILER-NO-WASTE-ALARM-36.md): No profiler, perf ledger or gate flagged idle cores, work done for maps that are removed, or growth of the image (fixed)
- [BUILD-REUSE-SCRATCH-UNDECLARED-33](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-SCRATCH-UNDECLARED-33.md): A rerun with --reuse-from reused 1 of 33 stages: the builder's scratch folder made the first stages non-reusable (fixed)
- [CHIM-HEAP-INT-MATMUL-36](bugs/CHIM-HEAP-INT-MATMUL-36.md): The CHIM heap scan multiplies 0/1 tables as int32: numpy's plain loop, 6 of 24 cores, about 60 s per check (fixed)
- [SEYDA-READ-SLOW-31](bugs/SEYDA-READ-SLOW-31.md): dev1 Seyda crossings read slower than the ov700 test image (fixed)

### Bugs by family

Every bug from v0.0.30 on belongs to one family (bugs with a shared cause or area); the families and their shared rules are in [bug tracking](bugs/README.md#families).

#### Vivec preview frame and its joins to the world (`vivec-frame-edge`)

- [DOCS-VIVEC-FRAME-PLAN-36](bugs/DOCS-VIVEC-FRAME-PLAN-36.md): The docs contradict each other on how Vivec goes onto CHIM (one frame vs eight district frames vs the coordinate range) (open, medium)
- [TOWN-EDGE-UNBUILT-32](bugs/TOWN-EDGE-UNBUILT-32.md): Leaving a town into an unbuilt neighbour drops the player into a bare world map (open, high)
- [VIVEC-ARENA-FLOATING-NPC-32](bugs/VIVEC-ARENA-FLOATING-NPC-32.md): A Vivec resident stands at the end of a walkway by the Telvanni canton with sky drawn below his feet (open, low)
- [VIVEC-ARENA-FRAME-EDGE-32](bugs/VIVEC-ARENA-FRAME-EDGE-32.md): Neighbouring canton bodies end at the Arena frame edge, in view (open, medium)
- [VIVEC-ARENA-HANDOFF-32](bugs/VIVEC-ARENA-HANDOFF-32.md): The Arena frame's handoff area reaches into neighbouring cantons; its frame edge can be in view (open, medium)
- [VIVEC-ARENA-WATER-FALL-32](bugs/VIVEC-ARENA-WATER-FALL-32.md): The sea ends at the Arena canton edge: sky below the horizon, and the player falls out of the area through the water (open, medium)
- [VIVEC-CANTON-SKY-HOLE-32](bugs/VIVEC-CANTON-SKY-HOLE-32.md): Sky shows through a Vivec canton wall seen from below (open, low)
- [VIVEC-DISTANT-BRIDGES-32](bugs/VIVEC-DISTANT-BRIDGES-32.md): Distant bridges between the Vivec cantons are not drawn (open, low)

#### Collision shapes and climbing (stairs, ramps, walkways) (`stairs-collision`)

- [BUILD-ACTOR-GATE-CHIM-FRAMES-36](bugs/BUILD-ACTOR-GATE-CHIM-FRAMES-36.md): The actor placement gate ignores CHIM frame residents: a CHIM-only build fails with no actors, and a blocked frame resident passes silently (open, high)
- [BUILD-FLORA-STAIR-PLATES-BUDGET-36](bugs/BUILD-FLORA-STAIR-PLATES-BUDGET-36.md): The stair-plate collision rules raised a legacy open-world map past the BSP clip-node limit: world-flora failed on vf0779 (33,172 aggregate clipnodes > 32,767) (open, critical)

- [BUILD-STAIR-FLAG-INERT-32](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-FLAG-INERT-32.md): The stair rule build option does nothing in v0.0.32 (open, low)
- [CHIM-HULL-STAIR-EDGE-33](bugs/CHIM-HULL-STAIR-EDGE-33.md): With the qbsp-compiled terrain hull on Balmora's regular ground, one flight of stairs fails at a chunk edge (open, high)
- [CHIM-SEYDA-EXPANDED-STAIRS-40](bugs/CHIM-SEYDA-EXPANDED-STAIRS-40.md): Expanded Seyda CHIM frame fails stair walks at two original references (open, critical)
- [CHIM-VOS-TEMPLE-WALKWAY-41](bugs/CHIM-VOS-TEMPLE-WALKWAY-41.md): Vos walkway stair starts inside the Velothi temple's collision and fails the stair gate (open, high)
- [COLLISION-CONVEX-LOSS-32](bugs/COLLISION-CONVEX-LOSS-32.md): Convex collision proxies lose and invent authored surfaces (open, high)
- [COLLISION-HULL-CHAINS-33](bugs/COLLISION-HULL-CHAINS-33.md): 183 brush models across the island collide through standing-hull chains 512 to 31,659 clipnodes deep (open, high)
- [COLLISION-NC-FLAGS-32](bugs/COLLISION-NC-FLAGS-32.md): Morrowind no-collision and editor-marker flags (NC, NCC, MRK, AvoidNode) are ignored by the mesh converters (open, medium)
- [COLLISION-RCN-SCOPE-32](bugs/COLLISION-RCN-SCOPE-32.md): Authored collision meshes (RootCollisionNode) are used by some converters only (open, medium)
- [COLLISION-ROUTE-NO-SPLIT-36](bugs/COLLISION-ROUTE-NO-SPLIT-36.md): Routed standing hulls of dense flora (kelp, kreshweed) stay one chain 277-305 clipnodes deep: the router finds no cut, so the cell stays hull-pending (open, medium)

- [COLLISION-STAIR-SLOPE-32](bugs/COLLISION-STAIR-SLOPE-32.md): Convex collision proxies make stairs unclimbable (open, medium)

- [COLLISION-TRACE-LIMIT-ROUTED-ROOM-36](bugs/COLLISION-TRACE-LIMIT-ROUTED-ROOM-36.md): The tool collision tracer stops on the Vivec Arena Pit's routed hull (visit limit), so its start spot cannot be traced (open, low)
- [SEYDA-BLOCK-31](bugs/SEYDA-BLOCK-31.md): Invisible obstacle blocks the path on a Seyda Neen slope (open, medium)

- [AW-LEASE-NO-GROW-36](bugs/AW-LEASE-NO-GROW-36.md): A running build's core lease did not grow back when gates ended, and its worker pools never grow (open, medium)
- [BUILD-CACHE-ABSOLUTE-PATHS-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-ABSOLUTE-PATHS-33.md): Stage fingerprints contain absolute paths, so a moved workspace or checkout reuses nothing (open, low)
- [BUILD-CACHE-CHIM-UNITS-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-CHIM-UNITS-33.md): The chim stage's fingerprint changes whenever the CHIM unit cache gains units, so the stage is never reused (open, medium)
- [BUILD-CACHE-CLOSURE-WIDE-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-CLOSURE-WIDE-33.md): Stage fingerprints count every module any imported module could import, so unrelated edits rebuild most stages (open, low)
- [BUILD-CACHE-NO-CUTOFF-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-NO-CUTOFF-33.md): A rebuilt stage whose outputs come out unchanged still rebuilds every stage after it (open, low)
- [BUILD-CACHE-OVERBROAD-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-OVERBROAD-33.md): CHIM builds rerun the scene chain after merges that cannot change its outputs (open, medium)
- [BUILD-CACHE-PER-WORKSPACE-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-PER-WORKSPACE-33.md): Per-file caches live in each build workspace, so a build on a new volume with --reuse-from converts everything again (MiniWind media 1,195 s instead of seconds) (open, high)
- [BUILD-CACHE-TRACE-PYTHONPATH-33](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-TRACE-PYTHONPATH-33.md): The stage read trace refused reuse of a reused run when another checkout was on PYTHONPATH (open, low)
- [BUILD-CHIM-FRAME-SWAP-LATE-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-FRAME-SWAP-LATE-36.md): The image step builds 335 legacy maps of cells already on CHIM, then removes them before packing (open, medium)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAGE-IDLE-START-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAGE-IDLE-START-36.md): The chim stage of the 270-frame coast build spends its first 35-45 minutes on 1-4 cores while it holds 12-24 workers (open, medium)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAGE-SERIAL-TAIL-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAGE-SERIAL-TAIL-36.md): After the parallel world build the chim stage ran its validation, heap gate and zone walk on one core (about 10 minutes of the 270-frame coast build) (open, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STATS-SERIAL-TAIL-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STATS-SERIAL-TAIL-36.md): The chim stage ended with its statistics validating the whole CHIM world a second time, on one core with no output (7 min 44 s on 273 frames) (open, medium)
- [BUILD-CHIM-UNIT-HULL-KEY-33](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-UNIT-HULL-KEY-33.md): CHIM unit cache ignores the standing-hull form: a --model-hull chain build reused routed-hull model units (open, high)
- [BUILD-DOOR-REFERENCE-SERIAL-33](bugs/BUILD-DOOR-REFERENCE-SERIAL-33.md): The door step reads the whole master once per destination cell (open, medium)
- [BUILD-ENGINE-SOURCE-KEY-BROAD-36](bugs/BUILD-ENGINE-SOURCE-KEY-BROAD-36.md): Any engine source edit changes the keys of the chim and world-flora stages (open, medium)
- [BUILD-ENV-FINGERPRINT-GLOBAL-33](bugs/BUILD-ENV-FINGERPRINT-GLOBAL-33.md): The stair rule setting invalidates the reuse cache of every build stage, including stages that never read it (open, low)
- [BUILD-EXCLUDE-STAGE-CLOSURE-33](bugs/BUILD-EXCLUDE-STAGE-CLOSURE-33.md): The quick test build table entered every conversion stage's fingerprint through the stage scheduler (open, medium)
- [BUILD-FLORA-FALLBACK-RETRY-35](bugs/BUILD-FLORA-FALLBACK-RETRY-35.md): The routed-hull fallback of a flora overlay could not retry: the failed try's candidate folder was looked up from the wrong place (open, medium)
- [BUILD-HAND-CATALOG-SERIAL-32](bugs/BUILD-HAND-CATALOG-SERIAL-32.md): The hand-catalog stage bakes every race and sex one after another (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IDLE-STAGES-33](bugs/BUILD-IDLE-STAGES-33.md): Scene-chain stages leave most of the workers they hold idle (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-AUDIO-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-AUDIO-SERIAL-36.md): The image step puts the audio payload in one file at a time while 22 of 24 cores sit idle (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-CHIM-MAPS-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-CHIM-MAPS-SERIAL-36.md): The image step writes the CHIM region cells' .chim maps one by one on half a core, silently (open, high)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-FRAME-MAP-HEAP-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-FRAME-MAP-HEAP-SERIAL-36.md): The image step's CHIM frame-map heap check runs on one core: 66 s to 4.5 min per build while 23 cores idle (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-NOT-INCREMENTAL-33](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-NOT-INCREMENTAL-33.md): The image step redoes every per-map pass and the whole pack on every build (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-SERIAL-32](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-SERIAL-32.md): The image step runs on one core for about 23 minutes per pass (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-UNDERUSED-32](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-UNDERUSED-32.md): Parts of the image step still run serially or leave most CPUs idle (open, medium)
- [BUILD-INPUTCHECK-SLOW-32](bugs/BUILD-INPUTCHECK-SLOW-32.md): The game data reference check takes over 12 minutes through Docker (open, medium)
- [BUILD-JOBS-RESOLVE-PER-STAGE-32](bugs/BUILD-JOBS-RESOLVE-PER-STAGE-32.md): With automatic jobs, stages of one build plan can get different worker counts (open, low)
- [BUILD-MINIWIND-FINGERPRINT-ENGINE-33](bugs/BUILD-MINIWIND-FINGERPRINT-ENGINE-33.md): Every build stage's source fingerprint took in the engine sources through the MiniWind module (open, medium)
- [BUILD-MINIWIND-STAGE-CLOSURE-33](bugs/BUILD-MINIWIND-STAGE-CLOSURE-33.md): The MiniWind plan puts the CHIM builder modules into every conversion stage's fingerprint (open, medium)
- [BUILD-NESTED-POOL-ALLOWANCE-33](bugs/BUILD-NESTED-POOL-ALLOWANCE-33.md): Pool workers open their own pools sized to the whole stage's allowance (open, medium)
- [BUILD-NPCLOD-PATH-ARG-34](bugs/BUILD-NPCLOD-PATH-ARG-34.md): The first --npc-lod on build stopped at its area stage: a Path in the command broke build-state.json (open, medium)
- [BUILD-OPTIMIZER-CACHE-KEY-BROAD-36](bugs/BUILD-OPTIMIZER-CACHE-KEY-BROAD-36.md): Regression: the image step's BSP optimizer pass cache reused 13 of 2,658 maps; its key covers the whole image tool, so any image-code change redoes every map (open, high)
- [BUILD-ORDERED-WINDOW-33](bugs/BUILD-ORDERED-WINDOW-33.md): The ordered worker pool idles behind one slow item, and hands long items out last (open, medium)
- [BUILD-PARTITION-GROWTH-UNGATED-36](bugs/BUILD-PARTITION-GROWTH-UNGATED-36.md): Nothing gates the number of world partitions or the growth of the image (open, medium)
- [BUILD-PIPELINE-UNDERUTILIZATION-36](bugs/BUILD-PIPELINE-UNDERUTILIZATION-36.md): Umbrella: several builder phases run on one core while the rest of the machine idles, and some do work the build no longer needs (open, high)
- [BUILD-PRERENDERED-PRUNE-ORDER-33](bugs/BUILD-PRERENDERED-PRUNE-ORDER-33.md): Prerendered store prune picks the superseded entry by folder name when two entries share a timestamp (open, low)
- [BUILD-PROFILE-JOBS-START-ONLY-33](bugs/BUILD-PROFILE-JOBS-START-ONLY-33.md): The build profile compares a stage's cores with the workers it started with, not the ones it held (open, medium)
- [BUILD-PROGRESS-ETA-BASIS-36](bugs/BUILD-PROGRESS-ETA-BASIS-36.md): The live build progress took a stage's expected time from earlier runs of any size: dev3's chim stage was expected at 154 s and ran 1 h 48 min (open, medium)
- [BUILD-REUSE-KEYS-TOO-BROAD-35](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-KEYS-TOO-BROAD-35.md): A whole build reused no stage, and the cost was not visible before the build started (open, medium)
- [BUILD-REUSE-TMP-UNDECLARED-33](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-TMP-UNDECLARED-33.md): A stage that runs while a reused stage is copied becomes non-reusable (open, medium)
- [BUILD-SCHEDULER-EVEN-SHARE-33](bugs/BUILD-SCHEDULER-EVEN-SHARE-33.md): The stage scheduler splits the workers evenly between running stages, so the longest stage gets no more than the shortest (MiniWind media on 1 of 4 workers for 1,195 s) (open, medium)
- [BUILD-SCHEDULER-JOBSHARE-32](bugs/BUILD-SCHEDULER-JOBSHARE-32.md): The stage scheduler fixes a stage's worker share when it starts (open, medium)
- [BUILD-SCHEDULER-LOWBUDGET-33](bugs/BUILD-SCHEDULER-LOWBUDGET-33.md): With a small --jobs budget the stage scheduler starts nothing when more branches are ready than the budget (busy loop) (open, medium)
- [BUILD-SILENT-PHASES-36](bugs/BUILD-SILENT-PHASES-36.md): Builder phases run silent: no start/end timing, no progress, no heartbeat; the CHIM stats step is a single-core tail (open, medium)
- [BUILD-STAGE-START-SHARE-33](bugs/BUILD-STAGE-START-SHARE-33.md): A pooled stage that starts while others run gets one worker as its start value (open, medium)
- [BUILD-STAIR-WALK-BORDER-COPIES-41](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-WALK-BORDER-COPIES-41.md): The CHIM stair gate walks every frame's whole border, so each cell's flights are walked many times and unreachable copies can fail a build (open, high)
- [BUILD-STAIR-WALK-SLOW-33](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-WALK-SLOW-33.md): The image step's stair walk spent most of its time in interpreted collision traces and brute-force headroom tests (1,751 s of the rc1c image step) (open, medium)
- [BUILD-STATS-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-STATS-SERIAL-36.md): The CHIM stats step runs serially and silently on one core (open, medium)
- [BUILD-STORAGE-DUPLICATES-33](bugs/BUILD-STORAGE-DUPLICATES-33.md): Build storage keeps full duplicate copies of images, stage outputs and packages, and nothing removes them (open, high)
- [BUILD-UNDERUSE-GATE-36](bugs/BUILD-UNDERUSE-GATE-36.md): The profiler did not flag long underused phases: 7 min 44 s of CHIM statistics on 1 of 24 cores and 77 s of zone walk on 1 core showed nowhere (open, medium)
- [BUILD-WINDOWS-DOCKER-SLOW-31](bugs/BUILD-WINDOWS-DOCKER-SLOW-31.md): Docker builds and disk-image steps on Windows folders are slow (open, medium)
- [CHIM-EMPTY-MODEL-REPORT-40](bugs/CHIM-EMPTY-MODEL-REPORT-40.md): Terrain-only CHIM validation crashes while reporting the largest scenery model (open, high)
- [CHIM-LEGACY-CHAIN-33](bugs/CHIM-LEGACY-CHAIN-33.md): CHIM builds still run the legacy exterior chain: frame maps read the legacy region maps, and the open world is built (open, medium)
- [CHIM-PVS-SLOW-33](bugs/CHIM-PVS-SLOW-33.md): Seyda Neen's CHIM visibility rows take 5.5 minutes and are rebuilt whenever the worker count changes (open, medium)
- [CHIM-STAIR-GATE-WORLD-RELOAD-36](bugs/CHIM-STAIR-GATE-WORLD-RELOAD-36.md): The CHIM stair gate's workers read the whole CHIM world again every time they switch frames (about 14.5 s per read on the 273-frame world) (open, high)
- [CHIM-STAIRGATE-SLOW-33](bugs/CHIM-STAIRGATE-SLOW-33.md): The CHIM stair gate takes over 10 minutes on the Balmora and Vivec Arena world (open, medium)
- [MINIWIND-NOT-MINUTES-33](bugs/MINIWIND-NOT-MINUTES-33.md): A reused MiniWind build took about two hours instead of minutes (open, high)
- [NPC-BAKED-WHOLE-DUPLICATION-33](bugs/NPC-BAKED-WHOLE-DUPLICATION-33.md): Every NPC appearance is baked whole: shared body parts are converted again for each actor, and equipment cannot change (open, medium)
- [AW-CONCURRENT-BUILDS-OVERSUBSCRIBE-36](bugs/AW-CONCURRENT-BUILDS-OVERSUBSCRIBE-36.md): Concurrent master-builder builds oversubscribe the machine: a gate step fails with 'cp: Cannot allocate memory' (fixed, high)
- [AW-LEASE-MEMORY-DEADLOCK-36](bugs/AW-LEASE-MEMORY-DEADLOCK-36.md): Memory leases deadlocked builds: a 44 GiB build never fit while any gate held its 6 GiB, so builds queued for hours (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-HASH-UNDERUSED-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-HASH-UNDERUSED-36.md): The image step hashes 34,592 payload files on 3 of 24 cores (one file per pool task) (fixed, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-HEAP-AUDIT-UNDERUSED-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-HEAP-AUDIT-UNDERUSED-36.md): The image step's world map heap audit estimates 170 maps on 5.3 of 24 cores (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-SERIAL-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-STAIR-WALK-SERIAL-36.md): The image step's stair walk runs one map per worker: a single heavy room keeps one core busy for 13-19 minutes while the others sit idle (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-PROFILER-NO-WASTE-ALARM-36](bugs/BUILD-PROFILER-NO-WASTE-ALARM-36.md): No profiler, perf ledger or gate flagged idle cores, work done for maps that are removed, or growth of the image (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-STAGE-INTERRUPT-ABSORBED-36](bugs/BUILD-STAGE-INTERRUPT-ABSORBED-36.md): The stage wrapper absorbed Ctrl-C: a stage that handled the interrupt and returned 0 counted as passed, and the build went on (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-PROFILE-SECTIONS-MISSING-36](bugs/BUILD-PROFILE-SECTIONS-MISSING-36.md): Long builder phases printed no elapsed time and recorded no sections: the chim stage's 44-minute stair gate was visible only from cache file times (closed, medium)

#### Build reuse keys and output attribution (`build-cache-reuse`)

- [BUILD-CACHE-OWNER-FAILS-STAGE-34](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-OWNER-FAILS-STAGE-34.md): A cache entry owned by another user failed a release build four minutes in, instead of being rebuilt or caught before any stage (open, high)
- [BUILD-CACHE-PROVENANCE-41](bugs/BUILD-CACHE-PROVENANCE-41.md): Some cache entries record no time or no versions: pool keys from CHIMport have empty versions and commit, world-terrain units and stage records have no creation time or version (open, medium)
- [BUILD-CELL-PROGRESS-KEY-BUGS-35](bugs/BUILD-CELL-PROGRESS-KEY-BUGS-35.md): Every bug registration stopped builds at the reuse preflight: the cell-progress key held all of docs/ (open, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-KEY-UNDERDECLARED-35](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-KEY-UNDERDECLARED-35.md): The CHIM stage's fingerprint left out the tracker code it ran (8 files, 2 functions): a wrong-reuse risk (open, high)
- [BUILD-DIAGNOSTIC-INPUT-REFUSED-36](bugs/BUILD-DIAGNOSTIC-INPUT-REFUSED-36.md): The cell-progress stage was never reusable: it reads the CHIM stage's statistics report, which counted as a forbidden diagnostic read (open, medium)
- [BUILD-ENGINE-KEY-SDK-33](bugs/BUILD-ENGINE-KEY-SDK-33.md): The engine stage is never reused: the Amiga SDK is not part of its key (open, low)
- [BUILD-FLORA-RECEIPT-SECONDS-36](bugs/BUILD-FLORA-RECEIPT-SECONDS-36.md): The world flora receipt held the stage's wall time, so a rerun with the same inputs never wrote the same receipt (open, medium)
- [BUILD-GALLERY-JSON-KEY-ORDER-35](bugs/BUILD-GALLERY-JSON-KEY-ORDER-35.md): NPC gallery outputs differed between two builds only in JSON key order (cache vs fresh results) (open, medium)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-NO-RESUME-33](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-NO-RESUME-33.md): A late failure in the image step reruns the whole image step, and the failing check could have run in its first seconds (open, high)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-STALE-PYTHONPATH-35](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-STALE-PYTHONPATH-35.md): Builder container images put an old builder copy on PYTHONPATH (open, high)
- [BUILD-KEY-FUNCTION-DEFAULTS-TOP-36](bugs/BUILD-KEY-FUNCTION-DEFAULTS-TOP-36.md): Adding a function to a shared module changed the key of every stage that imports it, although no stage runs the new code (open, high)
- [BUILD-KEY-OVERBROAD-33](bugs/BUILD-KEY-OVERBROAD-33.md): A new bug page or release file list change rebuilds interior, census, harvest and the CHIM stages: an import search path counted as a read of every file under tools/ (open, high)
- [BUILD-NPC-BAKE-CACHE-KEY-40](bugs/BUILD-NPC-BAKE-CACHE-KEY-40.md): New NPC shell fallback dependencies are absent from the model cache key (open, critical)
- [BUILD-OUTPUTS-NOT-REPRODUCIBLE-33](bugs/BUILD-OUTPUTS-NOT-REPRODUCIBLE-33.md): A stage that runs again never matches its old outputs (tool logs, timings, run paths), so every stage after it is rebuilt (open, high)
- [BUILD-PASS-KEY-OVERBROAD-36](bugs/BUILD-PASS-KEY-OVERBROAD-36.md): Image-pass and unit cache keys changed with code no pass runs: a new function in a shared module and edits to the cache's own key code rekeyed every character head preview twice (Vivec stage A try2 and try3: 275 of 275 recomputed) (open, high)
- [BUILD-PASS-KEY-RULE-NO-CARRYOVER-36](bugs/BUILD-PASS-KEY-RULE-NO-CARRYOVER-36.md): A pass key rule change carried no entries written by older trees: dev4 recomputed 2,372 optimizer maps and the exterior sky maps with code and outputs identical to dev3 (open, high)
- [BUILD-POOL-APPLY-READ-MISS-35](bugs/BUILD-POOL-APPLY-READ-MISS-35.md): In a pool-mode build every reused stage was refused as the next reuse source: the reuse step's read of tools/storage_pool.py counted as a stage input (open, high)
- [BUILD-POOL-ARG-UNFINGERPRINTED-35](bugs/BUILD-POOL-ARG-UNFINGERPRINTED-35.md): The balmora stage was never reusable once the shared storage pool grew past 512 MiB: its --npc-model-pool folder was hashed as an input (open, high)
- [BUILD-POOL-READONLY-SCENE-WRITE-35](bugs/BUILD-POOL-READONLY-SCENE-WRITE-35.md): A pool-mode resume failed in 17 s: npcs could not rewrite its copy of seyda.bsp (copytree kept the pool's read-only mode) (open, high)
- [BUILD-RESUME-HAZARD-STATIC-35](bugs/BUILD-RESUME-HAZARD-STATIC-35.md): A resume failed in 'area': the rebuild hazard counted files of a reused stage that had run again after all (open, high)
- [BUILD-RESUME-OUTPUTS-DIFFER-35](bugs/BUILD-RESUME-OUTPUTS-DIFFER-35.md): Every v0.0.35 resume refused the stages after a rerun scene-chain stage as 'outputs differ' without comparing them (open, high)
- [BUILD-REUSE-ATTRIBUTION-33](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-ATTRIBUTION-33.md): Two stages that ran at the same time were both refused reuse when one wrote files under a folder the other only reads (open, high)
- [BUILD-REUSE-CASCADE-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-CASCADE-36.md): A fix in CHIM-native town code made 23 of 36 stages rebuild: the interior stage reran with different outputs and 5 rebuilds were unexpected (open, high)
- [BUILD-REUSE-CUTOFF-MODE-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-CUTOFF-MODE-36.md): Early cutoff compared file modes with their write bits: a stage reused by hard link (read-only) never matched the same bytes written fresh, so 10 stages after dev3's bsp rerun were refused (open, high)
- [BUILD-REUSE-PLAN-ENV-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-PLAN-ENV-36.md): The standalone reuse plan reports environment changes the real build does not have (open, medium)
- [BUILD-REUSE-RECORD-RETIRED-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-RECORD-RETIRED-36.md): Early cutoff and the reuse audit lost their records when an older run was retired: stages with byte-identical outputs counted as changed (open, high)
- [BUILD-SCENE-DIAGNOSTIC-COPY-35](bugs/BUILD-SCENE-DIAGNOSTIC-COPY-35.md): Scene-chain stages were never reusable: copying the previous scene (logs included) counted as reading another stage's diagnostics (open, medium)
- [BUILD-SCHEDULER-TABLE-KEY-35](bugs/BUILD-SCHEDULER-TABLE-KEY-35.md): A new row in the build scheduler's stage table rebuilds stages that never read it: the table counted as import-time code of every stage (open, high)
- [BUILD-SEYDA-BOUNDED-RUN-PATHS-36](bugs/BUILD-SEYDA-BOUNDED-RUN-PATHS-36.md): The actor-contact stage wrote its run folder path into 67 Seyda Neen region records, so its outputs differed in every run folder (open, high)
- [BUILD-STAIR-KEY-BOOKKEEPING-36](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-KEY-BOOKKEEPING-36.md): Stair walk keys count the progress lines (stair_progress.py) and the record bookkeeping (stair_records.py): an edit to either rekeys every walk (open, medium)
- [BUILD-STAIR-PASS-NOT-KEPT-41](bugs/BUILD-STAIR-PASS-NOT-KEPT-41.md): A frame's passed stair walk is not reused when the build it ran in failed, and the walker key changes with converter code (open, high)
- [BUILD-SURVEY-KEY-CONFIG-35](bugs/BUILD-SURVEY-KEY-CONFIG-35.md): World survey, world terrain, world UI and CHIM stage keys counted every file under config/: a path built from a loop name read as the whole folder (open, high)
- [BUILD-SURVEY-NOT-REPRODUCIBLE-33](bugs/BUILD-SURVEY-NOT-REPRODUCIBLE-33.md): The world-survey stage writes its wall time into its outputs, so a rerun never has the same outputs (open, medium)
- [CACHE-NO-PROVENANCE-36](bugs/CACHE-NO-PROVENANCE-36.md): Cache and pool entries were keyed by SHA-256 but did not record when, by which run, from which version and commit they were made, and some recorded no output hash (open, medium)
- [CHIMPORT-NO-SHARED-POOL-35](bugs/CHIMPORT-NO-SHARED-POOL-35.md): The CHIMporter kept its own unit store beside the shared hashed storage pool: no pool flag, every run folder a private copy (open, medium)
- [CHIMPORT-POOL-CROSS-MOUNT-36](bugs/CHIMPORT-POOL-CROSS-MOUNT-36.md): CHIMport run folder and the storage pool on two mounts of one volume: hard links fail across the mounts, so pooled units were copied, not linked (open, medium)
- [CHIMPORT-RETRY-AUDIT-FAILED-36](bugs/CHIMPORT-RETRY-AUDIT-FAILED-36.md): CHIMport --retry-failed never reran cells that converted but failed an audit (memory fit, doors, hull chains): the runner marks them done (open, medium)
- [MWAD-WALK-ORDER-35](bugs/MWAD-WALK-ORDER-35.md): Input check results depend on directory listing order (open, low)
- [BUILD-REUSE-AUDIT-WITHOUT-REASON-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-AUDIT-WITHOUT-REASON-36.md): The reuse audit and preflight called a stage rerun because its old record was short a replaced file UNEXPECTED, although the reason explains it (fixed, low)
- [BUILD-REUSE-REDUCED-RECORD-36](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-REDUCED-RECORD-36.md): A stage reused from a run that had itself reused it came back without the files a later stage replaced there, so that later stage failed when it ran again (fixed, critical)
- [BUILD-REUSE-SCRATCH-UNDECLARED-33](bugs/BUILD-REUSE-SCRATCH-UNDECLARED-33.md): A rerun with --reuse-from reused 1 of 33 stages: the builder's scratch folder made the first stages non-reusable (fixed, high)

- AW25-05: Console typed only uppercase letters and shifted digits (open, medium)

- [ENGINE-COM-TOKEN-UNBOUNDED-35](bugs/ENGINE-COM-TOKEN-UNBOUNDED-35.md): COM_Parse wrote tokens of any length into the 1 KiB com_token (open, medium)
- [ENGINE-LOADGAME-SYSERROR-35](bugs/ENGINE-LOADGAME-SYSERROR-35.md): A damaged Quake save stopped the program (Sys_Error) and its strings were read without a width (open, low)
- [ENGINE-MAP-NAME-OVERFLOW-35](bugs/ENGINE-MAP-NAME-OVERFLOW-35.md): map, changelevel, connect and load copied a name of any length into fixed buffers (open, medium)
- [ENGINE-WRITE-OPEN-SYSERROR-35](bugs/ENGINE-WRITE-OPEN-SYSERROR-35.md): A write to a full or write-protected disk (screenshot) stopped the program (open, medium)
- [KEYS-AMIGA-EDIT-32](bugs/KEYS-AMIGA-EDIT-32.md): Amiga Del arrived as F11; FS-UAE sent Home/End as keypad ( and Help (open, medium)

- VIEW-PITCH-CENTER-29: Legacy automatic pitch centering during free-look (open, low)
- AW25-04: M/N switched to the desktop, including while typing in the console (fixed, medium)
- [UI-MAP-CLICK-DRAGS-SCREEN-36](bugs/UI-MAP-CLICK-DRAGS-SCREEN-36.md): A click on the game screen with the Amiga qualifier (held, or latched by a crashed program) drags the game screen down and reveals the AmigaDOS screen behind it (fixed, high)

- [AUDIO-03](bugs/AUDIO-03.md): Audio glitch at the post-movie load into the Jiub opening scene (WinUAE) (open, medium)

- AUDIO-CLOCK-29: Long audio DMA gaps lose elapsed playback time (open, medium)

- [AUDIO-HOST-LOAD-33](bugs/AUDIO-HOST-LOAD-33.md): Music and sound crackle when the emulator shares a fully loaded host CPU (open, low)
- AUDIO-LOAD-29: OST crackles under heavy loading (open, medium)
- [AUDIO-LOGO-31](bugs/AUDIO-LOGO-31.md): Music crackles while the startup logo plays (WinUAE) (open, medium)
- [AUDIO-NEWGAME-30](bugs/AUDIO-NEWGAME-30.md): Music crackles when confirming New Game (WinUAE) (open, low)
- [AUDIO-STOP-CHANNEL-RANGE-39](bugs/AUDIO-STOP-CHANNEL-RANGE-39.md): Stopping an entity sound skips half the dynamic mixer slots (open, medium)

- [CHIM-SEYDA-HUNK-GAP-33](bugs/CHIM-SEYDA-HUNK-GAP-33.md): Seyda Neen's CHIM map leaves less than the 2 MiB Hunk-gap safety at the default zone (open, high)
- [CHIM-SEYDA-MEMORY-33](bugs/CHIM-SEYDA-MEMORY-33.md): Seyda Neen's CHIM ring was modelled larger than Balmora's: the irregular ground's routed standing hull (open, high)
- [CHIM-STRIDER-RING-33](bugs/CHIM-STRIDER-RING-33.md): The closed-hull strider (MESH-LOD-OPEN-SEAMS-33, variant E) puts CHIM Balmora's active ring over the heap budget (open, high)
- [CHIM-ZONE-BUDGET-33](bugs/CHIM-ZONE-BUDGET-33.md): The engine's default CHIM zone does not hold the active ring of Seyda Neen or of Balmora's south-west corner (open, medium)
- [CHIM-ZONE-RESERVE-EARLY-33](bugs/CHIM-ZONE-RESERVE-EARLY-33.md): chim_reserve_kib is checked before the map's actors and precaches load, so it cannot keep the Hunk gap (open, medium)
- CRASH-01: Heap exhaustion on sn012 after Hors travel from Jiub name entry (open, critical)

- [HEAP-MODEL-SUM-32](bugs/HEAP-MODEL-SUM-32.md): Summing per-object costs overestimates a map's heap (11 % median, 39 % worst) (open, low)
- HUNK-RESERVE-SN012-29: Dev4 first-presented sn012 reserve below 2 MiB target (open, medium)
- [LOADER-STAGING-PEAK-32](bugs/LOADER-STAGING-PEAK-32.md): Map loading stages most lumps in temporary memory before decoding, raising the heap peak (open, medium)
- [MAP-UNUSED-HULL2-32](bugs/MAP-UNUSED-HULL2-32.md): Shipped maps carry about 1.1 MB of collision data for a hull the engine never uses (open, medium)
- [NPC-ANIM-MEMORY-33](bugs/NPC-ANIM-MEMORY-33.md): Full animation kit models are 2.2 times the idle models; four Balmora residents exceed the 512 KiB alias staging buffer (open, medium)
- [VIVEC-HEAP-31](bugs/VIVEC-HEAP-31.md): Dense Vivec maps exceed the loader heap budget once mappings and extents no longer stop them (open, high)

- MEM-TOWN-02: Bounded Balmora maps exceeded the modeled map heap limit (fixed, medium)

- BALMORA-CAPACITY-005: Bounded Balmora maps exceed the 600-entity limit (open, high)

- [FLAMES-CAP-31](bugs/FLAMES-CAP-31.md): Static flames above 128 per map are silently dropped (open, medium)
- FLORA-ENTITY-001: Dense vegetation exceeds the per-map entity reserve (open, medium)
- FLORA-RESERVE-002: Two world flora maps exceed storage and clipnode reserves (open, medium)

- [SEYDA-LOAD-HANG-30](bugs/SEYDA-LOAD-HANG-30.md): Rare freeze during a Seyda Neen region load (open, critical)
- [STREAM-FFS-SEEK-32](bugs/STREAM-FFS-SEEK-32.md): Random 16 KiB reads on FFS run at 0.44 MB/s and take most of the CPU (open, medium)
- [SEYDA-READ-SLOW-31](bugs/SEYDA-READ-SLOW-31.md): dev1 Seyda crossings read slower than the ov700 test image (fixed, medium)

#### Rendering cost and visibility (`render-performance`)

- AW-20260928-07: Slow opening exterior / excessive residency (open, medium)
- [CHIM-SLOWCPU-FRAMETIME-33](bugs/CHIM-SLOWCPU-FRAMETIME-33.md): Balmora on CHIM runs at about 1 frame per second on a slow 68040 (cycle-exact emulation) (open, low)
- [CHIM-TRACE-TAIL-33](bugs/CHIM-TRACE-TAIL-33.md): A few CHIM collision traces visit thousands of clipnodes (open, medium)
- [ENGINE-C2P-ROWSTRIDE-35](bugs/ENGINE-C2P-ROWSTRIDE-35.md): The display conversion ignored the bitmap row stride: a screen whose rows are padded for the fetch mode rendered skewed (open, medium)
- [ENGINE-CEILF-PER-EDGE-36](bugs/ENGINE-CEILF-PER-EDGE-36.md): R_EmitEdge calls the C library ceil twice per edge (open, low)
- [ENGINE-DEAD-FIXEDPOINT-GRADIENTS-36](bugs/ENGINE-DEAD-FIXEDPOINT-GRADIENTS-36.md): The 68040 build converts every drawn surface's gradients to fixed point that nothing reads (open, low)
- [ENGINE-FLOATTIME-DIV64-35](bugs/ENGINE-FLOATTIME-DIV64-35.md): Sys_FloatTime made two 64-bit library divisions per call to build seconds and microseconds (open, low)
- [ENGINE-FPCR-DANCE-36](bugs/ENGINE-FPCR-DANCE-36.md): Every float-to-int conversion in the engine saves, changes and restores the FPU control register (open, medium)
- [ENGINE-UPSTREAM-ASM-UNUSED-36](bugs/ENGINE-UPSTREAM-ASM-UNUSED-36.md): The engine is built without AmiQuake's 68k assembly: spans, fixed point and maths run as C at -O1 (open, high)
- [ENGINE-UPSTREAM-SPANS8-SPRITE-LOOP-36](bugs/ENGINE-UPSTREAM-SPANS8-SPRITE-LOOP-36.md): Upstream AmiQuake's assembly D_DrawSpans8 has the shape of the sprite span loop; it never runs at the default d_subdiv16 1 (open, low)
- [ENGINE-VID-UPDATE-FIRST-RECT-35](bugs/ENGINE-VID-UPDATE-FIRST-RECT-35.md): VID_Update converted only the first rectangle of the update list (open, medium)
- MODAL-WORLD-29: Head/race and journal backgrounds consume world work (open, medium)
- [NPC-TARGET-REDUNDANT-31](bugs/NPC-TARGET-REDUNDANT-31.md): NPC targeting runs 2-4 times per frame and checks every NPC (open, medium)
- [RENDER-BMODEL-FRAGMENTS-32](bugs/RENDER-BMODEL-FRAGMENTS-32.md): Brush models spanning many terrain leaves are clipped face by face down the terrain BSP (open, high)
- [RENDER-EDGECACHE-SEYDA-32](bugs/RENDER-EDGECACHE-SEYDA-32.md): No edges are reused between frames in Seyda Neen views (open, medium)
- [RENDER-SURFCACHE-THRASH-32](bugs/RENDER-SURFCACHE-THRASH-32.md): Seyda Neen views rebuild the surface cache every frame at a fixed camera (open, medium)
- [TOWN-VIS-OCCLUSION-31](bugs/TOWN-VIS-OCCLUSION-31.md): Converted buildings, rooms and rocks do not block Quake visibility (func_wall) (open, medium)

#### CHIM world streamer (`chim-streamer`)

- [BUILD-CHIM-HEAP-GATE-LATE-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-HEAP-GATE-LATE-36.md): The CHIM heap gate runs after the stair walk: a ring over the zone is found 69 minutes late (open, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-VALIDATE-LATE-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-VALIDATE-LATE-36.md): The CHIM world is validated only after the whole chim stage: a bad chunk is found 45 minutes in (open, high)
- [CHIM-ACTOR-RING-33](bugs/CHIM-ACTOR-RING-33.md): A scripted actor step could move the CHIM chunk ring to the actor (open, low)
- [CHIM-ACTORS-OUTSIDE-RING-33](bugs/CHIM-ACTORS-OUTSIDE-RING-33.md): Actors outside the CHIM ring have no terrain collision (open, medium)
- [CHIM-ALIAS-STATIC-HUNK-35](bugs/CHIM-ALIAS-STATIC-HUNK-35.md): Streamed alias statics load into the cache mid-map without being counted in the map's Hunk rest (open, medium)
- [CHIM-ANIM-TEXTURES-33](bugs/CHIM-ANIM-TEXTURES-33.md): Animated shared textures in CHIM show only their first frame (open, low)
- [CHIM-ARENA-MEMORY-33](bugs/CHIM-ARENA-MEMORY-33.md): The Vivec Arena does not fit the CHIM zone: canton bodies are single models of 1.4-2.5 MB (open, high)
- [CHIM-BALMORA-LIGHT-ROOM-33](bugs/CHIM-BALMORA-LIGHT-ROOM-33.md): Balmora's CHIM active ring has 2,528 bytes of headroom: no stored lighting fits (open, medium)
- [CHIM-BORDER-COLLISION-33](bugs/CHIM-BORDER-COLLISION-33.md): Collision near CHIM chunk borders ignores the neighbour chunk's ground (open, medium)
- [CHIM-BRUSH-BAD-DATA-35](bugs/CHIM-BRUSH-BAD-DATA-35.md): A damaged or truncated CHIM model or terrain image ends the game (Sys_Error) instead of failing that load (open, high)
- [CHIM-CHUNK-LOAD-FAIL-33](bugs/CHIM-CHUNK-LOAD-FAIL-33.md): Balmora chunks fail to load on CHIM, never recover, and leave holes without ground (open, high)
- [CHIM-COMPILED-HULL-SHORT-36](bugs/CHIM-COMPILED-HULL-SHORT-36.md): Compiled standing hulls lose pieces: terrain up to 3 units below the ground, Hlaalu houses up to 139 units (open, high)
- [CHIM-DETAIL-BUDGET-SEAM-UNAUDITED-36](bugs/CHIM-DETAIL-BUDGET-SEAM-UNAUDITED-36.md): The seam-tear audit does not measure the CHIM detail budgets: the Vivec statues at 900 of 5,451 triangles are not checked for opened seams (open, medium)
- [CHIM-DEV3-MAPLOAD-CRASH-36](bugs/CHIM-DEV3-MAPLOAD-CRASH-36.md): v0.0.36-dev3 image: the emulator exits about 2 s after a map load (JIT preset); the game stops advancing on the cycle-exact preset (open, critical)
- [CHIM-EFRAG-UNCAPPED-35](bugs/CHIM-EFRAG-UNCAPPED-35.md): CHIM placements link efrags without a cap: a large ring can reach the efrag pool limit and end the map (Host_Error) (open, high)
- [CHIM-EMPTY-POINT-HULL-36](bugs/CHIM-EMPTY-POINT-HULL-36.md): A CHIM model without collision has no point-hull node: the engine reads it as solid, or refuses it on load (open, critical)
- [CHIM-FAR-BAND-GAP-36](bugs/CHIM-FAR-BAND-GAP-36.md): From a CHIM area's border the ground ends at the frame edge: up to 476 units with no land before the fog plane (open, medium)
- [CHIM-FAR-OBJECTS-33](bugs/CHIM-FAR-OBJECTS-33.md): Houses between the CHIM ring and the legacy overlap depth are missing from the fogged horizon (open, medium)
- [CHIM-FAR-TERRAIN-33](bugs/CHIM-FAR-TERRAIN-33.md): A CHIM frame has no distant ground: beyond the active ring the land is missing (empty valleys, Vivec not visible from the world) (open, high)
- [CHIM-FERN-NCO-SOLID-38](bugs/CHIM-FERN-NCO-SOLID-38.md): Fire ferns marked NCO become solid in CHIM mesh conversion (open, medium)
- [CHIM-FRAME-COORD-RANGE-33](bugs/CHIM-FRAME-COORD-RANGE-33.md): One Vivec city frame does not fit Quake's coordinate range (network coordinates are 1/8-unit shorts) (open, medium)
- [CHIM-FRAME-GROUND-TRACE-38](bugs/CHIM-FRAME-GROUND-TRACE-38.md): CHIM image assembly exceeds collision traversal guard in cell 6,-5 (open, critical)
- [CHIM-FRAME-WORLD-BOUNDS-33](bugs/CHIM-FRAME-WORLD-BOUNDS-33.md): A CHIM frame map must carry an empty world whose bounds cover the frame (open, medium)
- [CHIM-FROZEN-ACTORS-33](bugs/CHIM-FROZEN-ACTORS-33.md): Frozen CHIM actors: projectiles stop mid-air and timers stop (open, medium)
- [CHIM-GRAFT-FAIL-NO-FLOOR-35](bugs/CHIM-GRAFT-FAIL-NO-FLOOR-35.md): A CHIM map whose frame world cannot start loses its far terrain too: the player may spawn over void (open, medium)
- [CHIM-GRAFT-REPACK-EMPTY-33](bugs/CHIM-GRAFT-REPACK-EMPTY-33.md): A frame world repack without room for the whole ring drops every chunk: the world vanishes and the player falls under Seyda Neen (open, high)
- [CHIM-HANDOFF-POPIN-36](bugs/CHIM-HANDOFF-POPIN-36.md): Most of a CHIM area's objects are not drawn from the legacy side and appear at the handover (Gnaar Mok, a Dwemer ruin) (open, medium)
- [CHIM-HARVEST-NAMING-33](bugs/CHIM-HARVEST-NAMING-33.md): A town-wide CHIM harvest catalogue does not fit the name and size limits (open, low)
- [CHIM-HARVEST-REMOVED-MAPS-33](bugs/CHIM-HARVEST-REMOVED-MAPS-33.md): A pure CHIM image stops at the save fingerprint: harvest catalogues of the removed town region maps have no matching map (open, high)
- [CHIM-HIDDEN-FACES-33](bugs/CHIM-HIDDEN-FACES-33.md): CHIM draws placed-model faces under the terrain that the recorded Seyda Neen region maps cull (open, low)
- [CHIM-HULL-CHAIN-COST-33](bugs/CHIM-HULL-CHAIN-COST-33.md): Large placed models collide through one long chain of convex pieces: a trace near the Arena canton walks about 6,600 planes (open, high)
- [CHIM-HULL2-33](bugs/CHIM-HULL2-33.md): CHIM terrain uses the player hull for large entities (open, medium)
- [CHIM-LAVA-RECOVER-38](bugs/CHIM-LAVA-RECOVER-38.md): Debug recovery fails to find a clear spawn after lava contact (open, low)
- [CHIM-LAVA-TERRAIN-AUDIT-37](bugs/CHIM-LAVA-TERRAIN-AUDIT-37.md): Terrain validation omits lava beds, surfaces and contents (open, high)
- [CHIM-LEAF-SPAN-33](bugs/CHIM-LEAF-SPAN-33.md): 160 of 1,488 Balmora placements span more than 16 leaves (open, medium)
- [CHIM-LIGHT-CONTENTS-33](bugs/CHIM-LIGHT-CONTENTS-33.md): Actor lighting and water contents ignore CHIM chunks (open, medium)
- [CHIM-MEASURE-EMPTY-FRAME-33](bugs/CHIM-MEASURE-EMPTY-FRAME-33.md): CHIM world measurement stops on a frame without models (every empty sea cell) (open, medium)
- [CHIM-MESH-PROFILE-SPLIT-36](bugs/CHIM-MESH-PROFILE-SPLIT-36.md): Seyda Neen converts the same meshes with other profiles than the other CHIM areas (texture size 64 against 32, no surface collision rule), so they are stored twice (open, low)
- [CHIM-MIPTEX-OFFSET-UNCHECKED-35](bugs/CHIM-MIPTEX-OFFSET-UNCHECKED-35.md): CHIM texture mip offsets are taken from disk unchecked: a wrong offset makes the rasterizer read past its block (open, medium)
- [CHIM-MODEL-ARENA-RESERVE-41](bugs/CHIM-MODEL-ARENA-RESERVE-41.md): Placed CHIM models reserve renderer nodes that their point-hull loader never allocates (open, high)
- [CHIM-MOLAG-MEMORY-38](bugs/CHIM-MOLAG-MEMORY-38.md): Tel Uvirith exterior cell (10,1) exceeds the CHIM active-ring memory budget (open, high)
- [CHIM-PACK-DIRS-33](bugs/CHIM-PACK-DIRS-33.md): CHIM pack directories are fully resident and would grow to about 400 KB for the island (open, medium)
- [CHIM-PACK-LRU-STREAM-35](bugs/CHIM-PACK-LRU-STREAM-35.md): A streamed model keeps a sector file handle the four-file cache may close: the next stream step reads a closed file (open, high)
- [CHIM-PAYLOAD-PARITY-33](bugs/CHIM-PAYLOAD-PARITY-33.md): The CHIM world misses the image step's edits to Balmora (harvest mushrooms, town flora) (open, high)
- [CHIM-PREDICT-VIVEC-LOW-36](bugs/CHIM-PREDICT-VIVEC-LOW-36.md): The CHIM memory predictor under-predicts every Vivec district frame by 28-54 %: Vivec architecture decodes to 1.3-2.3 times the bytes per triangle it was fitted on (open, medium)
- [CHIM-PVS-HOLLOW-33](bugs/CHIM-PVS-HOLLOW-33.md): Chunk visibility culls almost nothing in Balmora: houses have hollow collision shells (open, medium)
- [CHIM-READ-BUDGET-33](bugs/CHIM-READ-BUDGET-33.md): CHIM per-frame read budget can be exceeded by a whole lump (open, medium)
- [CHIM-REBUILD-COST-33](bugs/CHIM-REBUILD-COST-33.md): Each CHIM crossing rebuilds the whole frame world; the cost is not measured (open, medium)
- [CHIM-RECEIPT-COMMIT-33](bugs/CHIM-RECEIPT-COMMIT-33.md): CHIM world receipts do not record the commit the world was built from (open, low)
- [CHIM-RECIPE-APRON-SCOPE-41](bugs/CHIM-RECIPE-APRON-SCOPE-41.md): Regional aprons lose original-cell visual recipes and regress the memory gate (open, high)
- [CHIM-REGION-DIVIDED-CELLS-36](bugs/CHIM-REGION-DIVIDED-CELLS-36.md): Most converted land cells cannot switch to CHIM inside the open world: the legacy builder split them over several maps and a region frame replaces exactly one (open, high)
- [CHIM-REGION-MARGIN-STORAGE-36](bugs/CHIM-REGION-MARGIN-STORAGE-36.md): One-cell CHIM region frames store about 3.5 times their cell's terrain (the terrain-only margin) (open, medium)
- [CHIM-SEYDA-ACTOR-CONTACT-33](bugs/CHIM-SEYDA-ACTOR-CONTACT-33.md): rc1 image step refuses Seyda Neen's CHIM frame map: six actors stand 0.8-3.3 units off the CHIM ground (open, critical)
- [CHIM-STATIC-PLACE-HOST-ERROR-35](bugs/CHIM-STATIC-PLACE-HOST-ERROR-35.md): Bad flora data at CHIM chunk activation ended the session with zone locks held; the next map then stopped the program (open, medium)
- [CHIM-STATICS-FALLBACK-41](bugs/CHIM-STATICS-FALLBACK-41.md): A frame map with more than 512 streamed statics crashes when its CHIM world cannot load, instead of running without its chunks (open, medium)
- [CHIM-STITCH-SHORELINE-36](bugs/CHIM-STITCH-SHORELINE-36.md): A CHIM area in the legacy world has no shoreline refinement: steps of up to 28 units at its border (open, high)
- [CHIM-TERRAIN-GRAFT-33](bugs/CHIM-TERRAIN-GRAFT-33.md): CHIM chunk terrain is a brush entity, not part of the world tree, so it does not occlude (open, medium)
- [CHIM-TERRAIN-HULL-BEVELS-33](bugs/CHIM-TERRAIN-HULL-BEVELS-33.md): A standing box rests above the ground at convex CHIM terrain edges (open, medium)
- [CHIM-TEXTURE-SPECKS-33](bugs/CHIM-TEXTURE-SPECKS-33.md): CHIM-drawn surfaces show bright single-texel specks that legacy frames do not (open, medium)
- [CHIM-TILT-VARIANTS-SPLIT-33](bugs/CHIM-TILT-VARIANTS-SPLIT-33.md): Tilted placements each get their own model variant: 39,103 extra model variants on the base island, breaking store-once (open, high)
- [CHIM-TOWN-HEAP-ROUTED-HULL-36](bugs/CHIM-TOWN-HEAP-ROUTED-HULL-36.md): With the routed terrain hull for every chunk, Balmora and Seyda Neen rings no longer fit the CHIM zone (open, high)
- [CHIM-UNIT-CACHE-RACE-33](bugs/CHIM-UNIT-CACHE-RACE-33.md): Two processes storing the same CHIM unit at once: the second rename fails (open, medium)
- [CHIM-UNIT-FP-SOURCE-LAYOUT-33](bugs/CHIM-UNIT-FP-SOURCE-LAYOUT-33.md): The same mesh is converted again for every source stage: CHIM mesh unit fingerprints include archive offsets and texture list positions (open, high)
- [CHIM-VALIDATOR-ORDER-33](bugs/CHIM-VALIDATOR-ORDER-33.md): The CHIM validator walk depended on Python string hashing (open, low)
- [CHIM-VARIANT-YAW-DUPLICATES-36](bugs/CHIM-VARIANT-YAW-DUPLICATES-36.md): One CHIM model variant is stored several times: each frame builds it at the yaw of its own first placement (open, low)
- [CHIM-VIEW-FACES-33](bugs/CHIM-VIEW-FACES-33.md): Model faces dominate each Balmora view: the streamer alone does not fix frame rate (open, medium)
- [CHIM-VIEW-LEAF-NO-PVS-35](bugs/CHIM-VIEW-LEAF-NO-PVS-35.md): A view leaf without a visibility row draws everything, with no counter saying so (open, low)
- [CHIM-WINDOW-MOUNT-CROSS-CELL-33](bugs/CHIM-WINDOW-MOUNT-CROSS-CELL-33.md): A one-cell CHIM frame stops when a window placed in it mounts on a facade placed in the next cell (open, medium)
- [CHIM-WORLD-AUDIT-SCALING-33](bugs/CHIM-WORLD-AUDIT-SCALING-33.md): The CHIM world audits grow much faster than the world: the heap gate took 3.6 s for 171 frames, 473 s for 331, and over an hour for 844 (open, medium)
- [CHIM-WORLD-INDEX-FILE-LIMIT-36](bugs/CHIM-WORLD-INDEX-FILE-LIMIT-36.md): The engine refuses a CHIM world index with more than 4096 file rows; the dev3 coast world has 7,236, so every CHIM frame map fails (open, critical)
- [CHIM-ZONE-RING-THRASH-33](bugs/CHIM-ZONE-RING-THRASH-33.md): Balmora's chunk ring does not fit the default 6 MiB CHIM zone, so the cache thrashes (open, medium)
- [CHIM-ZONE-TMP-NOEXEC-33](bugs/CHIM-ZONE-TMP-NOEXEC-33.md): The CHIM zone walk gate cannot load its host library when /tmp is mounted noexec (open, high)
- [CHIM-ZONE-UNSATISFIABLE-EVICT-35](bugs/CHIM-ZONE-UNSATISFIABLE-EVICT-35.md): The CHIM zone evicts every unlocked block for a request no free run can hold (open, medium)
- [CHIMPORT-OCEAN-LAND-41](bugs/CHIMPORT-OCEAN-LAND-41.md): 98 seabed cells (land records entirely under water) are marked open ocean: CHIMport never plans them and the ocean admission refuses them (open, medium)
- [CHIMPORT-RETRY-LAVA-STALE-SOURCE-36](bugs/CHIMPORT-RETRY-LAVA-STALE-SOURCE-36.md): CHIMport --retry-failed cannot rerun a lava cell of the island run: its stored source stage predates the lava conversion (open, high)
- [BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-WALK-UNCACHED-36](bugs/BUILD-CHIM-STAIR-WALK-UNCACHED-36.md): CHIM frames are stair-walked again on every build: about 69 minutes for 270 frames (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-IMAGE-CHIM-NATIVE-TOWN-36](bugs/BUILD-IMAGE-CHIM-NATIVE-TOWN-36.md): The image step of a CHIM-native town read legacy region maps that are never built: the Bitter Coast test build failed at the end of its image step on maps/bc000.bsp (fixed, high)
- [BUILD-LEGACY-CHAIN-DEFAULT-36](bugs/BUILD-LEGACY-CHAIN-DEFAULT-36.md): Builds still prepared CHIM towns through the legacy region-map chain by default, and a retired flag (--legacy-area) was accepted with only a warning (fixed, high)
- [CHIM-FRAME-EDGE-EFRAG-CRASH-36](bugs/CHIM-FRAME-EDGE-EFRAG-CRASH-36.md): R_StoreEfrags: Bad entity type 270440 ends the game: a leaf link to an entity whose model is NULL (normal play in Seyda Neen; noclip past the Vivec frame edge) (fixed, critical)
- [CHIM-HEAP-INT-MATMUL-36](bugs/CHIM-HEAP-INT-MATMUL-36.md): The CHIM heap scan multiplies 0/1 tables as int32: numpy's plain loop, 6 of 24 cores, about 60 s per check (fixed, medium)

#### FPU and CPU behaviour (68040/68060) (`fpu-cpu`)

- [ENGINE-060-FSCC-41](bugs/ENGINE-060-FSCC-41.md): The engine has 263 fscc instructions (float comparisons as true/false), which the 68060 traps to its support library (open, medium)
- [ENGINE-FLOAT-SHORT-STORE-33](bugs/ENGINE-FLOAT-SHORT-STORE-33.md): 68040 engine build stored float-to-short conversions of the animation layout parser into the wrong group (open, medium)
- [ENGINE-FPSP-MISSING-31](bugs/ENGINE-FPSP-MISSING-31.md): Boot disk loads no 68040 FPU support library; emulator hides it (open, critical)
- [ENGINE-FPU-DATA-DECODE-33](bugs/ENGINE-FPU-DATA-DECODE-33.md): The 68040 FPU check reads a pointer table in the engine code section as fintrz instructions (open, medium)
- [ENGINE-FPU-UNIMPL-31](bugs/ENGINE-FPU-UNIMPL-31.md): Every-frame math traps on a real 68040 (sin+cos become cexp) (open, high)
- [ENGINE-FRAME-TIME-FLOAT-35](bugs/ENGINE-FRAME-TIME-FLOAT-35.md): The main loop held the seconds since start in floats: frame times quantise after hours of play (open, medium)
- [ENGINE-GAMMA-POW-35](bugs/ENGINE-GAMMA-POW-35.md): The gamma table called the C library pow, which executes FPU instructions a 68040 does not implement (open, low)
- [EXTENTS-FPU-RULE-31](bugs/EXTENTS-FPU-RULE-31.md): Surface extents and lightmap sizes depended on the FPU's arithmetic (open, high)
- [TEST-FPU-STRICT-JIT-31](bugs/TEST-FPU-STRICT-JIT-31.md): Emulator strict FPU mode is silently ignored while JIT is on (open, low)

#### Emulator measurement method (`benchmark-method`)

- [BENCH-AWBENCH-BUFFERS-32](bugs/BENCH-AWBENCH-BUFFERS-32.md): awbench reported the stale buffer count after AddBuffers (open, low)
- [BENCH-DISK-BYTES-32](bugs/BENCH-DISK-BYTES-32.md): FS-UAE moves disk bytes at about 90 MB/s, so it cannot price what a walk reads (open, low)
- [BENCH-FSUAE-FREQ-32](bugs/BENCH-FSUAE-FREQ-32.md): FS-UAE ignores uae_cpu_frequency in cycle-exact mode (open, low)
- [BENCH-FSUAE-JIT-HANG-32](bugs/BENCH-FSUAE-JIT-HANG-32.md): An FS-UAE JIT session hung with a Windows-folder hard file mounted (open, low)
- [BENCH-HOST-STORAGE-32](bugs/BENCH-HOST-STORAGE-32.md): Emulator disk timings with the hard file in a Windows folder measure the PC, not FFS (open, low)
- [BENCH-JIT-PROFILE-32](bugs/BENCH-JIT-PROFILE-32.md): Every frame-rate and load-time figure was measured with the emulator at host speed (open, low)
- [BENCH-SESSION-DRIFT-32](bugs/BENCH-SESSION-DRIFT-32.md): Emulated disk time drifts about 1.6x between FS-UAE sessions (open, low)
- [EMU-JIT-FAULT-ADDRESS-36](bugs/EMU-JIT-FAULT-ADDRESS-36.md): FS-UAE JIT reports fault addresses during the Vivec stage A boot smoke (open, low)

#### Disk images, partitions and launchers (`disk-image-layout`)

- [BENCH-FSUAE-PLAIN-HDF-32](bugs/BENCH-FSUAE-PLAIN-HDF-32.md): FS-UAE does not mount a plain 1.5 GiB partition hard file (open, low)
- [BUILD-HDF-BUFFERS-32](bugs/BUILD-HDF-BUFFERS-32.md): Hard disk buffer and MaxTransfer defaults depend on the disk type (open, medium)
- [BUILD-LAYOUT-GATE-AFTER-WRITE-33](bugs/BUILD-LAYOUT-GATE-AFTER-WRITE-33.md): The disk-layout gate ran only after the partitions and drives were written, and not on the dry-run image (open, low)
- [BUILD-WORLD-PARTITION-MOUNT-33](bugs/BUILD-WORLD-PARTITION-MOUNT-33.md): Full v0.0.33-dev1 image stops at 'Please insert volume AW_WORLD3': a partition starts beyond 2 GiB (open, high)
- [EMULATOR-TEMPLATES-WORLD-32](bugs/EMULATOR-TEMPLATES-WORLD-32.md): The static emulator templates list only the boot disk, not the world disk (open, medium)
- [FFS-DIRECTORY-HASH-32](bugs/FFS-DIRECTORY-HASH-32.md): Thousands of files in one directory are slow to open on a real FFS disk (open, medium)
- [IMAGE-ONE-HDF-TARGET-36](bugs/IMAGE-ONE-HDF-TARGET-36.md): The game ships on two drive images and three world partitions; the project target is the whole game on one HDF (open, medium)
- [WORLD-HDF-OVER-2GIB-32](bugs/WORLD-HDF-OVER-2GIB-32.md): The world hard disk image is 32,768 bytes over 2 GiB (open, medium)
- [WORLD-THIRD-PARTITION-32](bugs/WORLD-THIRD-PARTITION-32.md): The world image now needs a fourth partition (DW2); launchers were only used with three (open, medium)
- AW-20260928-17: FS-UAE 24-bit-addressing option rejected (fixed, medium)
- HDF-WRITER-01: Private image refresh left file-owned blocks marked free (fixed, medium)
- [AMIGA-DISK-2GIB-LIMIT-33](bugs/AMIGA-DISK-2GIB-LIMIT-33.md): Amiga disk limits: partitions below 2 GiB and starting below 2 GiB, files well under 2 GiB, drive images below 4 GiB (closed, medium)

#### Morrowind editions, archives and inputs (`game-data-editions`)

- [ASSETS-ARCHIVE-ORDER-32](bugs/ASSETS-ARCHIVE-ORDER-32.md): Asset readers ignore the Tribunal and Bloodmoon archives, so some textures are pre-expansion versions (open, high)
- [BUILD-EDITION-DIFFERENCES-32](bugs/BUILD-EDITION-DIFFERENCES-32.md): GOG and Steam editions produce different builds (fonts, loose files) (open, medium)
- [BUILD-EDITION-SKY-32](bugs/BUILD-EDITION-SKY-32.md): Night sky and sky palette outputs depend on the Morrowind edition (loose .tga read before archive .dds) (open, medium)
- [BUILD-EXPANSIONS-31](bugs/BUILD-EXPANSIONS-31.md): Tribunal and Bloodmoon cannot be converted with today's tools (open, medium)
- [BUILD-INPUTS-UNVERIFIED-32](bugs/BUILD-INPUTS-UNVERIFIED-32.md): The builder does not check user inputs (Morrowind data, Amiga libraries) against known versions (open, medium)
